{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01B2PAprpSABYVyQtcj9vUVY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01X8EL3mHvgjzXHHfnYHHZyo","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-45947","command":"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/colliforce-vla && python -c \"\nwith open('scripts/eval_safelibero.py', 'r') as f:\n    content = f.read()\n\nmerge_func = '''\ndef _merge_lora(model):\n    for module in model.modules():\n        for name, child in list(module.named_children()):\n            if isinstance(child, LoRALinear):\n                with torch.no_grad():\n                    delta = (child.scaling * (child.lora_A @ child.lora_B)).T\n                    child.base.weight.data += delta.to(child.base.weight.dtype)\n                setattr(module, name, child.base)\n\n\n'''\n\ncontent = content.replace('class SuffixOutCapture:', merge_func + 'class SuffixOutCapture:')\n\nwith open('scripts/eval_safelibero.py', 'w') as f:\n    f.write(content)\nprint('Done')\n\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":209,"cache_read_input_tokens":88488,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":209},"output_tokens":45,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0e27d80d-5004-45da-b44f-5c617d4808c2","uuid":"c7b78bbb-1fa3-4e8d-8f19-1236d586e06c"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01RYXjtFme8qvnAgfukeixU8","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Another error! The `_merge_lora` function failed with:\n```\nRuntimeError: Expected all tensors to be on the same device, but found at least two devices, cuda:0 and cpu!\n```\n\nThe issue is that after LoRA injection, the `lora_A` and `lora_B` parameters are loaded to `cuda:0` (from `torch.load(lora_path, map_location=\"cuda\")`), but the base weights might be on a different device. Or the base weight is on `cuda:0` and the LoRA params are on CPU.\n\n\nActually, I think I see the real problem now. The `_load_policy_a3` function loads the base model first (which goes to cuda:0 by default), then injects LoRA where the parameters get created on CPU initially, and when the LoRA weights are loaded from the file with `map_location=\"cuda\"` they end up on cuda:0, but then the merge operation tries to combine them with base weights that might still be on a different device.\n\nThe fix is to _merge_lora(model):\n    for module in model.modules():\n        for name, child in list(module.named_children()):\n            if isinstance(child, LoRALinear):\n                with torch.no_grad():\n                    delta = (child.scaling * (child.lora_A @ child.lora_B)).T\n                    child.base.weight.data += delta.to(child.base.weight.dtype)\n                setattr(module, name, child.base)'''\n\nnew = '''def _merge_lora(model):\n    for module in model.modules():\n        for name, child in list(module.named_children()):\n            if isinstance(child, LoRALinear):\n                with torch.no_grad():\n                    dev = child.base.weight.device\n                    lora_a = child.lora_A.to(dev)\n                    lora_b setattr(module, name, child.base)'''\n\ncontent = content.replace(old, new)\n\nwith open('scripts/eval_safelibero.py', 'w') as f:\n {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01HqvNHbrKyUDJQATMyEEsFP","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"thinking","thinking":"Another error! This time it's clear:\n\n```\nRuntimeError: Expected all tensors to be on the same device, but got mat2 is on cpu, different from other tensors on cuda:0 (when checking argument in method wrapper_CUDA_mm)\n```\n\nIn `LoRALinear.forward()`:\n```python\nlora_out = (x.float() @ self.lora_A @ self.lora_B) * self.scaling\n```\n\n`x` is on `cuda:0` (input tensor from the model forward pass), but `self.lora_A` is on `cpu`. \n\nI see the issue now—the LoRA parameters are being loaded onto CUDA with `map_location=\"cuda\"`, but then the `_inject_lora` function creates fresh Parameters on CPU in `LoRALinear.__init__` and copies the loaded values into them, which doesn't actually move them to the right device.\n\nThe problem is that `copy_()` preserves the destination tensor's device, so even though the source is on CUDA, the data gets copied back to CPU where the parameters were originally created. The fix is either to move the model to CUDA after loading the LoRA parameters, or to create the LoRA parameters on the same device as the base model from the start.\n\nI need to check whether the policy handles device movement internally—the `infer` method might move tensors to the model's device, but the newly injected LoRA parameters won't follow that movement since they're nn.Parameters that weren't moved. The solution is to add device synchronization in `_load_policy_a3` after loading the LoRA parameters, moving them to match the model's {"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_01MVXuki6fj6DjTW1JFrg7YY","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_019JseD8qwwQwgLy5X9fM5i9","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-45947","command":"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/colliforce-vla && python -c \"\nwith open('scripts/eval_safelibero.py', 'r') as f:\n    content = f.read()\n\n# Add device sync after LoRA param loading, before model.eval()\nold = '''        logging.info(\\\"Loaded %d LoRA params from %s\\\", len(lora_params), lora_path)\n    else:\n        logging.warning(\\\"lora_params.pt not found at %s\\\", lora_path)\n\n    model.eval()'''\n\nnew = '''        logging.info(\\\"Loaded %d LoRA params from %s\\\", len(lora_params), lora_path)\n    else:\n        logging.warning(\\\"lora_params.pt not found at %s\\\", lora_path)\n\n    device = next(model.parameters()).device\n    for p in lora_params:\n        p.data = p.data.to(device)\n    logging.info(\\\"Moved LoRA params to %s\\\", device)\n\n    model.eval()'''\n\ncontent = content.replace(old, new)\n\nwith open('scripts/eval_safelibero.py', 'w') as f:\n    f.write(content)\nprint('Done')\n\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1118,"cache_read_input_tokens":118868,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1118},"output_tokens":69,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0e27d80d-5004-45da-b44f-5c617d4808c2","uuid":"7aa191e3-9212-4c84-89c9-15bfaa70a7a6"}

{"type":"assistant","message":{"model":"claude-opus-4-6","id":"msg_014TkpBC5BgnzvaSuEhRnpj7","type":"message","role":"assistant","content":[{"type":"tool_use","id":"toolu_01P4bQ34gsoYWB9TfLVaZLRj","name":"mcp__agent-ml-research__ssh_execute","input":{"server":"westd-45947","command":"source /root/miniconda3/etc/profile.d/conda.sh && conda activate base && cd /root/colliforce-vla && python -c \"\nwith open('scripts/eval_safelibero.py', 'r') as f:\n    content = f.read()\n\n# Replace the device sync code with explicit CUDA move for LoRA modules\nold = '''    device = next(model.parameters()).device\n    for p in lora_params:\n        p.data = p.data.to(device)\n    logging.info(\\\"Moved LoRA params to %s\\\", device)'''\n\nnew = '''    for m in model.modules():\n        if isinstance(m, LoRALinear):\n            m.lora_A.data = m.lora_A.data.cuda()\n            m.lora_B.data = m.lora_B.data.cuda()\n    logging.info(\\\"Moved LoRA params to CUDA\\\")'''\n\ncontent = content.replace(old, new)\n\nwith open('scripts/eval_safelibero.py', 'w') as f:\n    f.write(content)\nprint('Done')\n\""},"caller":{"type":"direct"}}],"stop_reason":null,"stop_sequence":null,"stop_details":null,"usage":{"input_tokens":1,"cache_creation_input_tokens":1662,"cache_read_input_tokens":124194,"cache_creation":{"ephemeral_5m_input_tokens":0,"ephemeral_1h_input_tokens":1662},"output_tokens":63,"service_tier":"standard","inference_geo":"not_available"},"diagnostics":null,"context_management":null},"parent_tool_use_id":null,"session_id":"0e27d80d-5004-45da-b44f-5c617d4808c2","uuid":"c30f6ce7-50f6-4f16-96ae-9f067d3ee2f6"}
